# ABP_Unarmed Foot Placement MCP Results

Recorded requests and responses for Experiment 1's Foot Placement follow-up in
[AnimationBlueprintOperations.md](AnimationBlueprintOperations.md#follow-up-replace-control-rig-with-foot-placement).

Responses below retain the MCP envelope. The Foot node search is the one explicitly
marked excerpt; all other responses were captured directly during this run.
JSON strings inside `content[].text` and property `returnValue` fields are preserved.
The broad Component search and API schemas are intentionally retained in full.
